\doxysubsection{Detailed Description}
Module for complex k-\/point self-\/consistent DFT calculations. 

This module implements self-\/consistent field (SCF) calculations for complex wavefunctions with k-\/point sampling. It handles occupation number management, Hamiltonian diagonalization, and convergence control for both periodic and non-\/periodic systems. 

\label{doc-func-members}

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{checksum()}{checksum()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefreeelmod__complex_a559603ec5e9e2532e20461b01f4fa6b9} 
subroutine freeelmod\+\_\+complex\+::checksum}



Computes checksums of matrix elements for debugging and validation. 

This subroutine computes and prints checksums of various matrix elements including overlap, Hamiltonian, and density matrices. It is used for debugging and validation of the SCF calculations. Supports both serial and parallel execution with k-\/point sampling. 

Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{freeelmod__complex__kpoints_8f90_source_l02442}{2442}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{freeelmod__complex__kpoints_8f90_source}{freeelmod\+\_\+complex\+\_\+kpoints.\+f90}}.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{cycle\_complex()}{cycle\_complex()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefreeelmod__complex_ab6b35b23ca9a1519cfa5467fb50ff3ed} 
subroutine freeelmod\+\_\+complex\+::cycle\+\_\+complex}



Performs one iteration of the self-\/consistent field cycle for complex wavefunctions. 

This subroutine implements a single iteration of the SCF cycle including Hamiltonian update, diagonalization, occupation update, and density mixing. It supports both simple mixing (typeopt=2) and Anderson/\+Broyden acceleration (typeopt=4). Handles complex wavefunctions and k-\/point sampling. 

Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{freeelmod__complex__kpoints_8f90_source_l01381}{1381}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{freeelmod__complex__kpoints_8f90_source}{freeelmod\+\_\+complex\+\_\+kpoints.\+f90}}.

\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{cyclesteep\_complex()}{cyclesteep\_complex()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefreeelmod__complex_ad412305af17ef7075d422505e3d00b31} 
subroutine freeelmod\+\_\+complex\+::cyclesteep\+\_\+complex}



Performs steepest-\/descent optimization for complex wavefunctions. 

This subroutine implements steepest-\/descent optimization for complex wavefunctions in k-\/point calculations. It computes energy gradients and updates molecular orbitals using the steepest-\/descent method. Supports both serial and parallel (SCALAPACK) execution. 

Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{freeelmod__complex__kpoints_8f90_source_l02025}{2025}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{freeelmod__complex__kpoints_8f90_source}{freeelmod\+\_\+complex\+\_\+kpoints.\+f90}}.

{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefreeelmod__complex_ad1c2e1f2494e0a1d33d1105c651735cc} 
subroutine freeelmod\+\_\+complex\+::diagonalize\+\_\+hamiltonian (\begin{DoxyParamCaption}\item[{integer, intent(in)}]{iopt}{, }\item[{real(8), intent(inout)}]{lworkr}{}\end{DoxyParamCaption})}



Diagonalizes the Kohn-\/\+Sham Hamiltonian matrix. 

This subroutine diagonalizes the Kohn-\/\+Sham Hamiltonian matrix to obtain eigenvalues and eigenvectors. It handles both real and complex wavefunctions, supports LSDA calculations, and includes parallel (SCALAPACK) execution. The eigenvectors are stored in molecorb and molecorbdo arrays. 
\begin{DoxyParams}[1]{Parameters}
\mbox{\texttt{in}}  & {\em iopt} & Option flag for diagonalization type \\
\hline
\mbox{\texttt{in,out}}  & {\em lworkr} & Workspace size for LAPACK routines \\
\hline
\end{DoxyParams}

\Hypertarget{namespacefreeelmod__complex_a186e162cac7ed8e6912d2a7ad15582da}\index{freeelmod\_complex@{freeelmod\_complex}!self\_consistent\_run@{self\_consistent\_run}}
\index{self\_consistent\_run@{self\_consistent\_run}!freeelmod\_complex@{freeelmod\_complex}}
\doxysubsubsection{\texorpdfstring{self\_consistent\_run()}{self\_consistent\_run()}}
{\footnotesize\ttfamily \label{namespacefreeelmod__complex_a186e162cac7ed8e6912d2a7ad15582da} 
subroutine freeelmod\+\_\+complex\+::self\+\_\+consistent\+\_\+run}



Performs self-\/consistent field calculation for complex k-\/points. 

This subroutine implements the main self-\/consistent field loop for complex wavefunctions with k-\/point sampling. It reads occupation numbers from files, performs Hamiltonian diagonalization, updates density and potential, and controls convergence. Supports both LSDA calculations and parallel execution. 

Definition at line \mbox{\hyperlink{freeelmod__complex__kpoints_8f90_source_l00064}{64}} of file \mbox{\hyperlink{freeelmod__complex__kpoints_8f90_source}{freeelmod\+\_\+complex\+\_\+kpoints.\+f90}}.
